   15 | 
   16 | type AnimatedPressableProps = Omit<
   17 |   PressableProps,
   18 |   'style' | 'onPressIn' | 'onPressOut'
   19 | > & {
   20 |   style?: PressableProps['style'];
   21 |   pressedStyle?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;
   22 |   cue?: UiCue | false;
   23 |   scaleTo?: number;
   24 |   idleBreath?: boolean;
   25 | };
   26 | 
   27 | // animatedPressableV1
   28 | // Reusable physical-button feedback without changing screen layout.
   29 | export function AnimatedPressable({
   30 |   children,
   31 |   style,
   32 |   pressedStyle,
   33 |   cue = 'tap',
   34 |   scaleTo = 0.986,
   35 |   idleBreath = false,
   36 |   onPress,
   37 |   disabled,
   38 |   ...props
   39 | }: AnimatedPressableProps) {
   40 |   const scale = useRef(
   41 |     new Animated.Value(1),
   42 |   ).current;
   43 | 
   44 |   // ljingleSemanticAliveR1:hardware
   45 |   const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();
   46 |   const breath = useRef(new Animated.Value(1)).current;
   47 | 
   48 |   useEffect(() => {
   49 |     breath.stopAnimation();
   50 |     breath.setValue(1);
   51 |     if (!idleBreath || reducedMotion) return;
   52 |     const animation = Animated.loop(Animated.sequence([
   53 |       Animated.timing(breath, { toValue:1.004, duration:3200, useNativeDriver:true }),
   54 |       Animated.timing(breath, { toValue:1, duration:3600, useNativeDriver:true }),
   55 |     ]));
   56 |     animation.start();
   57 |     return () => { animation.stop(); breath.stopAnimation(); breath.setValue(1); };
   58 |   }, [breath, idleBreath, reducedMotion]);
   59 | 
   60 |   const livingScale = idleBreath && !reducedMotion ? Animated.multiply(scale, breath) : scale;
   61 | 
   62 |   const handlePressIn = () => {
   63 |     if (reducedMotion) return;
   64 |     scale.stopAnimation();
   65 |     Animated.spring(scale, {
   66 |       toValue: scaleTo,
   67 |       damping: 24,
   68 |       stiffness: 360,
   69 |       mass: 0.42,
   70 |       useNativeDriver: true,
   71 |     }).start();
   72 |   };
   73 | 
   74 |   const handlePressOut = () => {
   75 |     if (reducedMotion) {
   76 |       scale.setValue(1);
   77 |       return;
   78 |     }
   79 |     scale.stopAnimation();
   80 |     Animated.spring(scale, {
   81 |       toValue: 1,
   82 |       damping: 24,
   83 |       stiffness: 360,
   84 |       mass: 0.42,
   85 |       useNativeDriver: true,
   86 |     }).start();
   87 |   };
   88 | 
   89 |   return (
   90 |     <Animated.View
   91 |       style={{
   92 |         transform: [{ scale: livingScale }],
   93 |       }}
   94 |     >
   95 |       <Pressable
   96 |         {...props}
   97 |         disabled={disabled}
   98 |         onPressIn={handlePressIn}
   99 |         onPressOut={handlePressOut}
  100 |         onPress={(event) => {
  101 |           if (cue) {
  102 |             void playUiCue(cue);
  103 |           }
  104 | 
  105 |           onPress?.(event);
  106 |         }}
  107 |         style={(state) => [
  108 |           typeof style === 'function'
  109 |             ? style(state)
  110 |             : style,
  111 |           state.pressed ? pressedStyle : null,
  112 |         ]}
  113 |       >
  114 |         {children}
  115 |       </Pressable>
  116 |     </Animated.View>
  117 |   );
  118 | }
  119 |

 1750 | function HighlightedCatalogueTitle({
 1751 |   title,
 1752 |   terms,
 1753 | }: {
 1754 |   title: string;
 1755 |   terms: string[];
 1756 | }) {
 1757 |   const usefulTerms = terms
 1758 |     .map((term) => term.trim())
 1759 |     .filter(Boolean)
 1760 |     .sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length);
 1761 | 
 1762 |   if (usefulTerms.length === 0) {
 1763 |     return (
 1764 |       <Text
 1765 |         numberOfLines={1}
 1766 |         style={styles.resultTitle}
 1767 |       >
 1768 |         {title}
 1769 |       </Text>
 1770 |     );
 1771 |   }
 1772 | 
 1773 |   const escaped = usefulTerms.map((term) =>
 1774 |     term.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\const styles = StyleSheet.create({')
 1775 |   );
 1776 | 
 1777 |   let matcher: RegExp;
 1778 | 
 1779 |   try {
 1780 |     matcher = new RegExp(
 1781 |       '(' + escaped.join('|') + ')',
 1782 |       'gi',
 1783 |     );
 1784 |   } catch {
 1785 |     return (
 1786 |       <Text
 1787 |         numberOfLines={1}
 1788 |         style={styles.resultTitle}
 1789 |       >
 1790 |         {title}
 1791 |       </Text>
 1792 |     );
 1793 |   }
 1794 | 
 1795 |   const parts = title.split(matcher);
 1796 | 
 1797 |   return (
 1798 |     <Text
 1799 |       numberOfLines={1}
 1800 |       style={styles.resultTitle}
 1801 |     >
 1802 |       {parts.map((part, index) => {
 1803 |         const highlighted = usefulTerms.some(
 1804 |           (term) =>
 1805 |             normalize(part) === normalize(term)
 1806 |         );
 1807 | 
 1808 |         return (
 1809 |           <Text
 1810 |             key={index}
 1811 |             style={
 1812 |               highlighted
 1813 |                 ? styles.resultHighlight
 1814 |                 : undefined
 1815 |             }
 1816 |           >
 1817 |             {part}
 1818 |           </Text>
 1819 |         );
 1820 |       })}
 1821 |     </Text>
 1822 |   );
 1823 | }
